Dear Jeffrey, 
Next Monday, June 27. we'll celebrate the 5th anniversary of TEDTalks online. We launched in 2006 
videos and very modest goals -- and we've been amazed by what's happened since. As of this month, 
TEDTalks have been viewed 500 million times worldwide, and have been translated into 81 languages
To mark this milestone, we're taking a moment to honor the speakers who have offered the world (hell
the translators who have amplified them, and most of all, the TED community: You've lent us your cre
your passion and your support in spreading these ideas. 
Please join us next Monday for an early-summer, early-evening anniversary toast on the rooftop of ow
new(ish) office. 
TEDTalks 5th Anniversary Toast 
Monday. June 27. 2011 6:00 - 8:00 pm 
250 Hudson Street (South of Spring and North of Canal) 
RSVP to 5thAnniversarvOted.com

SENNA 
Thursday, June 23 6:30 pm 
Senna Is a documentary film about the life and death of Brazilian motor-racing 
champion, Ayrton Senna. The film starts from Senna's arrival in Formula One in the 
mid 1980s, and follows Senna's struggles both on track against his rival, French 
World Champion Alain Prost, and off it, against the internal politics of the sport.
